Method: 从生成矩阵构造正交表，建立OA及不可约正交表（IrOA）的Fourier型LU等价条件；针对素数字母表，确定所考虑线性OA的Fourier伙伴与其对偶码对应数组之间的关系。

Result: 给出了基于Fourier的OA与IrOA的LU等价条件；在素数字母表情形下，明确了线性OA的Fourier伙伴即其对偶码对应的数组，从而在LU等价与编码理论之间建立了显式联系；并识别出由特定线性码类导出的LU等价OA族。

Conclusion: 该工作将OA的LU等价问题转化为可借助编码理论分析的形式，为OA的分类及多体纠缠态的研究提供了新工具和联系。

Abstract: Orthogonal arrays (OAs) are combinatorial configurations with applications in experimental design, error-correcting codes, and quantum information. Local unitary (LU) equivalence provides a natural framework for classifying multipartite entangled states. Using the correspondence between OAs and quantum states, Goyeneche and Życzkowski [Phys. Rev. A, 2014, 90: 022316] posed the problem of determining when OAs are $LU$ equivalent. In this paper, we construct OAs from generator matrices and establish conditions for Fourier-based $LU$ equivalence of OAs and irredundant orthogonal arrays (IrOAs). For prime alphabets, we identify the Fourier partners of the linear OAs considered here with the arrays of their corresponding dual codes. This gives an explicit connection between $LU$ equivalence and coding theory. We also identify families of $LU$ equivalent OAs arising from specific classes of linear codes.

</details>


### [72] [Long-horizon autoformalization of a core theorem underlying MIP* = RE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19814)
*Sirui Lu,Ruixuan Deng,Yanqiao Zhu,Zhengfeng Ji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 FormalFlow 系统，在人类监督下协调 AI 证明智能体，通过共享蓝图引导嵌套式规划、证明与审查循环，完成 MIP* = RE 核心定理相关量子可靠性（quantum soundness）的 Lean 4 机器可验证形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标志性数学形式化工作通常需要专家团队耗费数年时间完成，成本高、周期长；长周期形式化中还存在命题漂移（statement drift）与证明组合（proof composition）的困难，亟需可扩展、可负担的验证路线。

Method: 借鉴软件工程的原则与实践，构建 FormalFlow 系统：以共享蓝图（shared blueprint）统一目标，引导嵌套的规划、证明与审查循环；由 AI 证明智能体在人类监督下协同工作，并在整个形式化过程中强化验证与审查机制。

Result: 完成经典低个体度测试（classical low individual-degree test）量子可靠性的 Lean 4 机器检查证明，该定理是 MIP* = RE 的核心基础；开发耗时 63 天，更大并行度可进一步缩短时间；最终代码库含 126,367 行 Lean 代码，全部由智能体生成；形式化过程中修正了侧条件（side conditions）与中间错误，同时在修正后的假设下保持了已发表的最终误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子复杂性提供了经过验证的基础，并展示了一条使小型团队也能以可负担成本完成重大研究证明验证的可行路径。

Abstract: Landmark mathematical formalizations have taken specialist teams years to complete. We present FormalFlow, a system that coordinates AI proving agents under human supervision to address statement drift and proof composition in long-horizon formalization. Drawing on software engineering principles and practices, it uses a shared blueprint to guide nested planning, proving and review loops. Agents strengthen verification and review throughout formalization. We completed a machine-checked Lean 4 proof of the quantum soundness of the classical low individual-degree test, a core theorem underlying MIP* = RE. Developing the proof took 63 days; greater parallelism could further reduce this time. The final library contains 126,367 lines of Lean code, all generated by agents. The formalization corrects side conditions and intermediate errors while preserving the published final error bound under corrected assumptions. This work provides a verified foundation for quantum complexity and demonstrates a route to affordable verification of major research proofs by small teams.

</details>


### [73] [Automated search for highly contextual Kochen-Specker proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19862)
*Axel Muller,Metod Saniga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于"超图"（上下文超图+反交换图）结构，先枚举反交换图并为其关联唯一超图支撑HS(G)，从而每图仅需检验一个候选，系统搜索高鲁棒性的可观测量型Kochen-Specker证明；在House of Graphs及至多24顶点的顶点传递图普查中，重新发现Peres-Mermin方阵、doily与Mermin五角星，并将容忍误差提升至ε=0.707（此前记录为0.424）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可观测量型Kochen-Specker证明的鲁棒性（以每上下文容忍误差ε=2d/|H|衡量）只取决于抽象的"超图"结构，而非具体的量子比特或Pauli标号。因此可在抽象结构层面进行搜索，以摆脱对具体Pauli实现方式的依赖，寻找更鲁棒的状态无关上下文性证明。

Method: 先枚举反交换图，再为每个图G关联携带其全部超图支撑的唯一超图HS(G)，使每个图只需检验一个候选结构。将该流程应用于House of Graphs数据库以及至多24顶点的顶点传递图普查。并对结果给出有限几何描述。进一步分析最佳构型的图论来源（线图与图的并）。

Result: 该流程重新发现了Peres-Mermin方阵、doily和Mermin五角星，并得到ε=0.707的构型，优于此前发表的0.424记录。最优构型多来自线图与图的并；作者证明图的每个完美匹配都是其线图的一个上下文，从而说明Peres-Mermin方阵与doily是两个无限族的前两个成员。

Conclusion: 在抽象超图层面搜索可观测量型Kochen-Specker证明是可行且高效的，可显著提升上下文性测试的鲁棒性。最优构型可由线图/图并表示，并在辛极空间中用卵形、双曲二次曲面与Fano平面给出有限几何刻画。

Abstract: Observable-based Kochen-Specker proofs are configurations of multi-qubit Pauli observables grouped into contexts whose products are plus or minus the indentity. Their robustness as state-independent contextuality tests can be measured by the tolerated error per context $\varepsilon = 2d/|H|$, where $d$ is the contextuality degree and $|H|$ the number of contexts. Since the degree depends only on an underlying abstract structure called the hypergram i.e. the pair formed by the context hypergraph and the anticommutation graph, the search for highly contextual proofs can be carried out on them instead, with no reference to qubits or to any particular Pauli labeling. We further exploit this by enumerating anticommutation graphs first, and then by associating to each graph $G$ the single hypergram carrying its entire hypergraph support $HS(G)$, so that exactly one candidate is examined per graph. Applied to the House of Graphs database and to censuses of vertex-transitive graphs on at most 24 vertices, this pipeline recovers the Peres-Mermin square, the doily and the Mermin pentagram, and yields configurations reaching $\varepsilon = 0.707$, against $0.424$ for the previous published record. The best configurations are predominantly those stemming from line graphs and unions of graphs; we explain the former by showing that every perfect matching of a graph is a context of its line graph, which exhibits the Peres-Mermin square and the doily as the first members of two infinite families. We close with finite geometric descriptions of the most striking configurations inside symplectic polar spaces, in terms of ovoids, hyperbolic quadrics and Fano planes.

</details>


### [74] [Quantum WalkScore: Benchmarking Quantum Computers on the Graph Nodefinding Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19931)
*Noé Olivier,Michel Nowak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantum WalkScore (QWS)，一种面向应用的可扩展基准，用于评估 NISQ 及未来容错量子计算机在执行离散时间量子行走与量子振幅放大以解决图节点查找问题时的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件向容错方向的发展提升了在近期量子平台上评估应用相关量子算法的需求，而现有缺乏面向此类应用任务的标准化、可扩展性能基准。

Method: QWS 通过给定成功概率阈值下能够找到指定目标节点的最大问题规模来进行打分，并给出完整的协议描述；同时基于无噪声模拟结果设计示例性参数选择方案，并在多代 IBM 真实量子处理器上进行实验评估。

Result: 提供了基于无噪声仿真的示例参数选择场景，并在多代 IBM 真实量子处理器上完成了 QWS 的实验验证。

Conclusion: QWS 为评估 NISQ 及容错量子计算机在图节点查找等应用任务上的性能提供了可扩展的基准框架，兼具协议规范性与实验可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing hardware toward fault-tolerance have increased interest in evaluating near-term quantum platforms on application-relevant quantum algorithms. In this work, we introduce Quantum WalkScore (QWS), a scalable application-oriented benchmark designed to assess the performance of NISQ and future fault-tolerant quantum computers in executing essential quantum routines --discrete-time quantum walks and quantum amplitude amplification-- to solve the graph nodefinding problem (marked-vertex search). QWS quantifies performance by scoring the largest problem size for which a designated target node can be found with success probability above a defined threshold. In addition to the complete protocol description, we provide example parameter-selection scenarios designed from noiseless simulation results and demonstrate QWS evaluation through experimental runs on multiple generations of IBM real quantum processors.

</details>


### [75] [Quantum Graph Convolutional Networks: Implementation and Trainability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19983)
*Paul San Sebastian Sein,Theodor Iosif,Tilen G. Limbäck-Stokin,Kin Ian Lo,Yidong Liao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于 Liao 等人的量子图神经网络 (QGNN) 框架，实现了 SGC 与 LGC 两种代表性架构，在开放基准图数据集和半监督任务上用量子模拟进行评测，结果显示量子模型以更少参数取得与经典基线相当的预测性能，并通过成本梯度分析与经典可模拟性研究界定了其可训练的任务范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN 在图结构数据上性能领先，但在大图上的训练与推理受内存约束和稀疏线性代数负载制约，成为可扩展性瓶颈。量子计算提供了另一套计算原语，有望改善图学习的可扩展性，因此需要实证检验量子图学习模型相对经典基线的实际表现与可训练性边界。

Method: 沿用 Liao 等人的 QGNN 框架，实现两种代表性架构：简化图卷积 (SGC) 与线性图卷积 (LGC)；在开放基准图数据集与半监督学习任务上采用量子模拟进行实验。将预测性能与优化行为同经典基线对比，并进行成本梯度 (cost gradient) 分析以识别模型可训练的任务，随后开展经典可模拟性 (simulability) 研究以确定电路在训练中保持稳健的区间。

Result: 量子模型（SGC、LGC）在更少参数下取得与经典基线具有竞争力的预测性能；成本梯度分析界定了所提模型可训练的任务集合；经典可模拟性研究给出了电路在训练过程中仍保持稳健的参数/任务区间。

Conclusion: 量子图卷积模型可作为经典 GNN 的参数高效替代方案，但其适用性受任务相关可训练性与经典可模拟性区间的约束；这为后续量子图学习的架构选择与规模扩展提供了实证依据与边界条件。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve state-of-the-art performance on graph-structured data, but training and inference on large graphs are often bottlenecked by memory constraints and sparse linear-algebra workloads. Quantum computing offers an alternative set of primitives that may improve scalability for graph learning. Building on the quantum graph neural network (QGNN) framework of Liao \textit{et al.}, this work implements two representative architectures --- the Simplified Graph Convolution (SGC) and Linear Graph Convolution (LGC) models --- and evaluates them on open benchmark graph datasets and semi-supervised learning tasks using quantum simulation. We compare predictive performance and optimization behavior against classical baselines, showing that the quantum models achieve competitive performance with fewer parameters. Finally, we present a cost gradient analysis that identifies the tasks for which the models showcased are trainable. This is followed by a classical simulability study to find regimes in which the proposed circuits remain robust during training.

</details>


### [76] [Semiclassical scaling of eigenstate thermalization in single-particle chaotic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20052)
*Yaoqi Ye,Xiao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Berry猜想研究单粒子混沌系统中实空间可观测量的非对角矩阵元，揭示其带状结构、特征带宽及与ETH熵标度的联系，并在量子台球中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间反演不变的单粒子混沌系统中实空间可观测量的非对角矩阵元行为，明确Berry猜想的动力学含义，并厘清单粒子本征态热化与多体ETH之间的关系。

Method: 从Berry猜想出发推导非对角方差半经典表达式，分析其带状结构；针对局域可观测量识别与粒子速度成反比的晚期时标对应的特征带宽；对陡壁约束系统推导其量级并引入动能依赖因子，以量子台球为例并采用广义四分之一Sinai台球进行数值验证。

Result: 非对角可观测量矩阵的带状结构自然涌现；局域可观测量存在与粒子速度倒数相关的晚期时标所定义的特征带宽；陡壁约束系统的预测幅度遵循ETH熵标度，但额外乘以与空间维度无关的动能依赖因子；数值验证与半经典标度一致。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了Berry猜想的动力学含义，并建立了单粒子本征态热化与多体ETH之间的比较，表明常规多体ETH未能涵盖该额外动能依赖因子。

Abstract: We study the off-diagonal matrix elements of real-space observables in time-reversal-invariant single-particle chaotic systems. By analyzing the semiclassical expression for the off-diagonal variance derived from Berry's conjecture, we show that the banded structure of the observable matrix emerges naturally. For local observables, we identify a characteristic bandwidth associated with a late-time timescale inversely proportional to the particle velocity. We further show that, for systems with steep-wall confinement, the predicted magnitude follows the entropy scaling of the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), multiplied by an additional kinetic-energy-dependent factor that is independent of spatial dimension and is not captured by conventional many-body ETH. We illustrate these results through a case study of quantum billiards and verify the semiclassical scaling numerically in a generalized quarter-Sinai billiard. Our results elucidate the dynamical implications of Berry's conjecture and provide a comparison between single-particle eigenstate thermalization and many-body ETH.

</details>


### [77] [Private communication from Pauli channels with no privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20133)
*Uthirakalyani G,Pritam Halder,David Elkouss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 两个各自私有经典容量为零的Pauli双量子比特信道，联合使用时可以传输私密信息，从而揭示了量子信道私有通信能力的非可加性（contextual）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典噪声信道的通信能力是确定的，而量子理论使信道能力具有情境性（contextual）。本文旨在探究私有经典容量是否具备非可加性——即单独无用、联合却有效的现象。

Method: 选取两个Pauli双量子比特信道：其一的环境可将接收端输出重构至矩阵转置精度；其二是反退化（antidegradable）信道。构造一个由Bell态的三秩混合（rank-three mixtures）组成的简单二进制编码来激活私密通信。核心构件是一个转置-反退化但非反退化的信道。

Result: 两个私有经典容量均为零的信道联合后可传输私密信息；当第二个信道替换为50%量子比特擦除信道时，得到类似结果。

Conclusion: 量子信道的私有经典容量不具有可加性，存在“超激活”式的联合效应；转置-反退化但非反退化的信道是关键机制。

Abstract: A channel capacity quantifies the communication capability of a noisy physical process. In contrast to communication channels in the classical world, quantum theory makes this capability contextual. We show that two Pauli two-qubit channels, each with zero private classical capacity, can be used to transmit private information when used together. One is a two-qubit Pauli channel whose environment can reconstruct the receiver output up to matrix transposition; the other is an antidegradable channel. We obtain a similar result when the second channel is the 50% qubit erasure channel. A simple binary code built from rank-three mixtures of Bell states activates private communication. The main ingredient in our construction is a transpose-antidegradable channel that is not antidegradable.

</details>


### [78] [Electrostatic Stabilization of Near-Surface Quantum Sensors via Dielectric Interface Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20137)
*Atharva Paranjape,Kathrin Küster,Olga Shevstova,Lisa Ebo,Toni Hache,Klaus Kern,Rainer Stöhr,Jörg Wrachtrup,Aparajita Singha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在金刚石表面引入TiO2介电涂层，抑制超高真空下的表面感应电场，稳定浅层NV色心的负电荷态，并显著提升其自旋相干与读出性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近表面量子缺陷的电荷态稳定性对纳米尺度传感至关重要，但在超高真空（UHV）环境下，表面诱导的能带弯曲会破坏与计量相关的电荷态（如NV⁻），使其难以稳定，制约了扫描探针兼容几何下的量子传感应用。

Conclusion: 介电屏蔽是一种稳健且可重复的策略，可用于在极端条件下、与扫描探针兼容的几何构型中工程化NV色心的电荷跃迁能量学，为近表面量子缺陷的电荷态稳定提供通用途径。

Through measurements on individually addressable NV centers, we demonstrate that a TiO2 coating on the diamond suppresses surface-induced electrostatic fields, yielding a 79% increase n NV- population and a 45% enhancement in NV-spin resonance contrast at room temperature. Coherent control measurements further reveal suppressed charge-state conversion dynamics. These results establish dielectric screening as a powerful and reproducible strategy to engineer charge transition energetics of NV centers in scanning-probe-compatible geometries under extreme conditions.

We then prove a fixed-reset no-go theorem: even a defective full tilted generator cannot generate a cumulative-count Jordan polynomial when the post-count ladder is scalar. Finally, we construct a three-reset monitored-Lindblad witness, minimal within irreducible nonnegative ladders, with a genuine subleading $Nλ^N$ threshold term and a coherence-tuned model in which a binary terminal effect retains the exact polynomial signature at a transversal transform-domain exceptional point. Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) underpins fault-tolerant quantum computing, but the circuits that realize QEC codes (encoders, logical state-preparation and gate routines, and syndrome-extraction schedules) are scattered across papers and one-off repositories in incompatible formats, often documented too sparsely to reconstruct or reuse. We present QECirc (https://qecirc.com/), a community-driven, open library that makes QEC circuits Findable, Accessible, Interoperable, and Reusable (FAIR). Each entry links to its underlying code, ships in Stim and OpenQASM with interactive visualizations, and is checked for correctness before publication. In short, where a catalog such as the Error Correction Zoo describes the codes, QECirc provides the concrete circuits that realize them.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

In this work, we present a measurement-free, unitary preparation of surface-code Pauli eigenstates tailored to neutral atoms. A bidirectional stabilizer-expanding CNOT cascade grows the patch from its middle line outward in depth $(d{+}3)/2$, while retaining fault distance $d$ in the protected error direction. Each layer maps to one collective atom move followed by a single global Rydberg pulse, with about one row pickup per two layers. We compile and simulate the resulting schedules with the open-source bloqade toolchain under a hardware-calibrated, circuit-level neutral-atom noise model. The bidirectional cascade achieves the lowest logical error rate at every distance, and is the only construction tested that operates below threshold for both logical states at the considered device-level noise.

</details>

Abstract: Early transformer fault diagnosis is challenged by nonlinear dissolved-gas interactions, overlapping fault signatures, and limited labeled data, while practical deployment further requires reliable performance under realistic computational constraints. This paper presents a simulation-driven modeling framework for dissolved gas analysis-based transformer fault diagnosis, in which a carefully engineered variational quantum classifier (VQC) is employed as the computational core and systematically analyzed through simulation. The framework integrates domain-aware feature modeling derived from Duval geometry with a lightweight two-qubit quantum representation, enabling nonlinear gas-interaction effects to be captured within a shallow parameterized circuit. A hybrid ZX-YY quantum feature map is designed to model non-commuting feature interactions, while a full-entanglement EfficientSU2 ansatz provides adequate expressive capacity under strict resource limits. Model behavior is evaluated using a comprehensive simulation pipeline including noise-aware circuit emulation, cross-dataset validation, and limited hardware-in-the-loop execution, allowing key effects of circuit depth, noise, and optimization strategy to be examined. Simulation results on benchmark dissolved-gas-analysis datasets demonstrate high diagnostic accuracy, strong generalization capability, and robustness to realistic noise levels with minimal quantum resources. The results highlight the effectiveness of simulation-informed modeling for practical transformer diagnostic applications, offering a reproducible and resource-efficient pathway for evaluating quantum-enhanced fault diagnosis methods.

</details>

*Ezra Acalapati,Elham Kashefi,Cica Gustiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子计算验证协议中"秘密独立性"假设在片上场景难以自动满足的问题，量化了残余秘密依赖对可验证计算规模的限制，并提出基于脉冲级量子控制的校准框架，使态制备同时具备高保真度与秘密独立性；在NV色心系统仿真中，秘密依赖降低超过一个数量级且对失谐和驱动幅度误差鲁棒。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证协议依赖"秘密独立性"假设，即影响态制备的噪声不依赖于指定所制备态的经典秘密。早期协议通过客户端与服务端的物理隔离来保证该假设；但在片上场景中，它成为硬件自身的属性，而高保真度器件并不自动满足这一点，因此需要一种能主动强制实现秘密独立性的方法。

Method: 作者首先将该要求定量化，推导出一个界限，刻画残余秘密依赖如何限制该框架内可验证计算的规模。基于此，提出一个校准框架，通过脉冲级量子控制强制实现秘密独立性，从而生成同时具备高保真度与秘密独立性的态制备。该框架完全在校准阶段运行，且与平台无关。

Result: 在氮-空位（NV）色心系统上通过仿真评估性能：与标准最优控制相比，所提框架将态制备的秘密依赖降低超过一个数量级，并对实验中常见的失谐与驱动幅度误差保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该校准框架为在片上场景下实现可扩展的可验证量子计算提供了一条实用路径，且因其平台无关性具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: Verification protocols provide cryptographic guarantees that the outcome of a delegated quantum computation is correct, a key requirement for scalable and trustworthy quantum computing. A central assumption underlying these protocols is secret independence: the noise affecting state preparation must not depend on the classical secrets that specify the prepared states. In earlier protocols this assumption was enforced by the physical separation of client and server; in the recent on-chip setting it becomes a property of the hardware itself, and one that high-fidelity devices do not automatically satisfy. We first make the requirement quantitative, deriving a bound that shows how residual secret dependence limits the size of any verifiable computation within this framework. Motivated by this, we introduce a calibration framework that enforces secret independence through pulse-level quantum control, producing state preparations that are simultaneously high-fidelity and secret-independent. We assess its performance on a nitrogen-vacancy centre system via emulation, showing that it reduces the secret dependence of the preparations by more than an order of magnitude compared to standard optimal control, and that it is robust against detuning and drive-amplitude errors commonly encountered in experiment. The framework operates entirely at the calibration stage and is platform-agnostic, offering a practical pathway towards verifiable quantum computing at scale.

</details>


### [85] [Phase-Flag Access and No-Go Constraints on Quotient-Space Real Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20226)
*Jeongho Bang,Kyoungho Cho,Kyunghyun Baek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析 Barrios Hita 等人提出的实数商空间量子力学表述，指出若将其"实旗帜"解读为可操作的真实自由度，则会与重叠可判定性（OD）及标准不可行定理冲突，并催生概率克隆、基于转向的通信与对数查询无序搜索等能力；作为受保护规范表述它等价于复数量子力学，作为可访问实数理论则脱离量子操作框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barrios Hita 等人 [PRL 136, 240202 (2026)] 提出实数上的商空间量子力学表述并认为复数仅是一种便利；前序分析 [arXiv:2607.05865] 表明当该构造与复数量子力学经验等价时，其实旗帜由隐藏的复结构保护。本文动机在于追问：若更强地按操作主义将旗帜解读为可访问的真实自由度，会产生什么后果。

Method: 概念与逻辑分析。将"可读或可相干操控的旗帜"视为固定每个射线代表元、从而提供相位约定的机制，并将其与重叠可判定性（OD，[arXiv:2601.14638]）及标准不可行定理对照；进一步把该资源提升为通用相位访问原语，推演其可实现的操作任务。

Result: 可访问的旗帜与 OD 及标准不可行定理直接冲突。可读或可相干操控的旗帜恰好提供了 OD 所指认的、实现未知态通用叠加所缺失的相位约定资源。一旦将其提升为通用相位访问原语，即可实现线性相关集的概率克隆、基于转向的通信（signaling）以及对数查询的无结构搜索。

Conclusion: 商空间构造有明确的后续解读：作为受保护的规范表述，它就是实记号下的标准复数量子力学；作为可访问的实数理论，它则脱离量子操作框架。

Abstract: Barrios Hita et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 136, 240202 (2026)] proposed a quotient-space formulation of quantum mechanics over the real numbers and concluded that complex numbers are only a convenience. A preceding analysis [arXiv:2607.05865] showed that, when the construction is empirically equivalent to complex quantum mechanics, its real flag is protected by a hidden complex structure $\hat{J}$ and its composition rule is the balanced tensor product over that structure. Here we ask what would follow from the stronger operational reading that the flag is an accessible real degree of freedom. The answer is a direct clash with overlap-determinability (OD), introduced in [arXiv:2601.14638], and with standard no-go theorems. A readable or coherently controllable flag fixes a representative of each ray, and hence supplies precisely the phase convention that OD identifies as the missing resource for universal superposition of unknown states. Once promoted to a generic phase-access primitive, this resource enables probabilistic cloning of a linearly dependent set, steering-based signaling, and logarithmic-query unstructured search. Thus the quotient construction has a sharp follow-up interpretation: as a protected gauge representation it is standard complex quantum mechanics in real notation; as an accessible real theory it leaves the quantum operational framework.

</details>


### [86] [Bandwidth-Tunable Quantum Light Source at 1.5 $μ$m](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20251)
*Wei Wu,Yun-Ru Fan,Ri-Yao Song,Si Shen,Zi-Chang Zhang,Hai-Zhi Song,Hao Li,Li-Xing You,Ping-He Wang,Yan-Yu Wei,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*
